  - **Q-v2 Ideas for after the step-16 gate (feature freeze, §5.0 item 7; owner 2026-10-24).** Each is tested later as a §7.1 Retraining challenger, never added before the gate. List (from the 2026-10-23 proposals, not built): earlier days of the week and earlier weeks as completed profiles; TPO POC/VA; poor highs/lows and excess; older naked POCs; profile shape (P / b / D / elongated); initiative vs responsive summaries; multi-expiration walls as registry levels; seconds from the touch to the Trinity / `es_at_level` overlap (owner 2026-10-24); ES volume baselines (an ES time-of-day normal like F9.10's, so ES cross-market levels could carry `xb_vol_rel` / `xb_delta_rel`; owner 2026-10-31, Q-step11c (3)); **a decision-time structure model** (owner 2026-10-24): rows at each `decision_ts`, features as of `decision_ts` including the overlap Trinity, the held/broke label measured from `decision_ts`, tested as a challenger to the touch-time structure model; **S&P and NDXP nodes as bounce sources for NQ (owner 2026-10-24, an extra test, outcome unknown):** QQQ nodes are the best for NQ, but because the indices are correlated a node on SPY and/or SPX (or NDXP) can make NQ bounce with no QQQ node there. Today NDXP nodes already create NQ events (§5.4 registry), while SPY/SPX nodes are features only (Trinity, `es_at_level`, S&P path obstacles) and never create an NQ event on their own, so a bounce at an S&P-only node is not an event. The later challenger would add an NQ event when ES touches a relevant SPY/SPX node band with no NQ zone within `zone_proximity` (the NQ touch price at that moment as a zero-width level, the S&P node's type, size and Trinity flags as features), scored with the same structure label, against the current system on unseen months (§7.1 Retraining). **Note for when it is built (owner 2026-10-24):** an S&P node has no NQ band, so the challenger needs its own NQ stop and far-edge (cross) rule; likely stop candidates 1, 3 and 5 only, as for area events (§5.4 Area events). The step-13 count (§12 step 13) sizes it first. **Added 2026-10-26 (owner):** **"node behind" features** for every level event: the largest relevant node behind the zone (on the far side from the approach) within 1 EM, its pct_of_king, its distance in EM and whether it is the (confirmed) King (owner read: with two large nodes stacked, the deeper one is the safer bounce). **Added 2026-10-25 (owner, from the Skylit-understanding questions):** a light beach-ball count (crossed, then back inside the band without a full reclaim; Q11); a session-level regime summary (Q16); a candidate-switch count, i.e. how contested the ceiling or King is (switches of the board leader in the last N minutes; Q25); **a per-map gamma flip level** (the strike where cumulative net gamma from the lowest strike changes sign, computed from each board) and the price's distance to it in EM, as a candidate regime feature. **Added 2026-11-01 (owner, §13 Q-step11g (1)):** the number of sessions that made the extreme at a retested extreme's price (the candidates merged within `retest_tol_ticks`), as a feature of that level. **Added 2026-11-05 (owner, §13 Q-density):** local level-density features: the number of zones within N pts of price (N proposed when built), the number of zones between the touch and the hold target, and the session's share of time so far inside a zone ± `zone_proximity`. **Added 2026-10-27 (owner; source: Garma's IB30 video; a note for after the step-16 gate only: no code, tests, config or `features_version` change now; each idea tested separately as a §7.1 Retraining challenger and kept only if it beats the live system on unseen months):**
    - **Idea 1, ES golden-zone overlap.** Draw the opening-range fib on ES with exactly the NQ rules (F10: 09:30 start, tradeable from 09:45, lock at 10:00, the 10:30 redraw and the orientation flips), on the session's ES front contract (F0.4), from ES's own traded high/low (wick extremes), in ES's own orientation by the swing rule. It uses the `golden_zone_flip` / `golden_zone_redraw_1030` values NQ ends up with after step 13; the ES fib is never tuned separately (a feature, never a trade setup). Features on NQ golden-zone events: `es_gz_orientation_agrees` (ES's own fib points the same way as NQ's); `es_in_golden_zone` (ES was inside its own golden zone, 0.5–0.79, in the same trade direction at some moment overlapping the NQ event, by the Trinity overlap rule F2.19: from the touch to t, data ≤ t, not invalidated at t; **false whenever ES's own orientation is opposite to NQ's, even if ES is inside its own golden zone: opposite directions are never agreement**), plus `es_fib_level_reached` (which ES fib level was reached); `es_retrace_nq_dir` (ES's position in its own opening range at t, measured with NQ's orientation and the owner's fib convention: 0 = ES's range extreme on the trade's target side (NQ's 0 level side), 1 = the opposite extreme (the 1.0 side), so 0.618 = ES at its .618; defined even when ES's own orientation disagrees with NQ's). Why: the owner's read is that the setup is stronger when NQ and ES are in their golden zones together; ES's own orientation is kept because disagreement between the two is information.
    - **Idea 2, intraday liquidity pools (equal highs / equal lows).** A pool is a price where stops are likely to sit because the session has made two or more highs (or lows) at nearly the same price; strict, so ordinary swing points don't count: swing highs/lows confirmed only after the fact (a high with N lower highs on each side, known only once those N bars have closed: no lookahead; N proposed when built); at least 2 swing highs (or lows) within `pool_tol_ticks` of each other (start value proposed when built, e.g. 2–4 ticks); between the touches price pulled back at least `pool_min_pullback` (points or ATR, start value proposed when built), so it is a real double top/bottom, not one wobbly high; still untouched (not traded through since the last touch); formed since the session start (18:00 ET), on NQ and separately on ES. **Features first, not levels:** pools create no events, zones or touches (owner: most swing points are noise; as features a useless pool just gets no weight and adds no events). Features on existing level events and pairs: the nearest untouched pool above and below price (distance in EM, number of touches, volume traded there); whether a pool was swept just before the entry (traded through and back inside within the §5.3 sweep window), how deep and how long ago; whether ES swept its own pool at an overlapping moment (e.g. an ES double-bottom sweep). Pools are also allowed as target candidates (§6.3), scored by the pair scorer like any other target. Only if the feature version clearly wins may a later challenger try pools as levels that create events.
    - **Idea 3, intraday regime switches (owner 2026-10-27; e.g. a range morning, then a trend afternoon; a note for after the step-16 gate only: no code, tests, config or `features_version` change now).** The day-type inputs and the EOD label (§5.3, §7.1) describe the session so far and the whole session, so a change of regime mid-day shows only slowly. Two candidates, each tested as a §7.1 Retraining day-type challenger and kept only if it beats the live model on unseen months: (1) **recent-window versions of the day-type inputs that are counted from the open**, each beside its full-day value: VWAP crosses (settled 5-minute rule, §13 Q-dtcross), VWAP side share, new-extreme skew, and range vs the expected move pro-rated to the window (`poc_crosses` over the window may be added as a challenger candidate only); the window length is not decided: tuned when built by day-blocked cross-validated log loss on months 1–4 over e.g. {30, 60, 120} minutes, with a pre-declared rule that keeps the middle value unless another is clearly better (its 95% interval of the difference excludes 0), or given to a LightGBM day-type challenger as several windows at once; (2) **a forward-looking day-type label**: from each snapshot t to the close, trend vs range by the same rules (§7.1 EOD label) applied to the rest of the session only; **added 2026-10-28 (owner, §13 Q-er):** ER from t to the close (5-minute bars, the `er_so_far` formula from snapshot t) as a candidate continuous target beside the trend/range label.
    - **Idea 4, map-role events (owner 2026-10-28, §13 Q-maprole; a note for after the step-16 gate only: no code, tests, config or `features_version` change now).** When the map makes a relevant QQQ/NDXP node the confirmed floor (or ceiling) by the F2.11 Q6 rule while the NQ price is inside its band, an event starts at once in that new role (at the `t_apply` of the board that confirms the switch; no cross, flip or re-arm needed), direction = the new role (floor → long, ceiling → short); price leaving the band in the new role's direction completes an audited hold, so it is traded as entry style (c) (the audit at the zone edge, §5.5). Must be reconciled with the Q9 inside-band protection first (today the same node can then be the confirmed ceiling and the confirmed floor at once, Q-maprole answer 0) and with the zone rule F5.6 (touch groups, a zone already in an event). Tested after the step-16 gate as a §7.1 Retraining challenger, kept only if it beats the live system on unseen months; sized first by the step-13 map-role conflicts count (§12 step 13).
    - **Idea 6, crosses at retested extremes (owner 2026-11-16; a note only).** Read-only on build b958fe6e42aa, months
      1–4 (members reconstructed, inside-first): touches at a retested extreme crossed 2+ times before the decision
      did no worse than those crossed 0–1 times (held 85.7% vs 83.0%, difference +2.6 pts [−2.8, +8.5]; bootstrap-pair
      net R −0.19 vs −0.07, −0.12 [−0.34, +0.10]; 95% day-block intervals). No change now; after 13d, repeat on months
      1–6 with the exact recorded members (Q-review-stack (b)).
    - **Idea 5, a cross margin for the chart-line types (owner 2026-11-10, §13 Q-step13a-2 (6); a note only: no code, tests, config or `features_version` change now).** The chart cross rule (a settled 1-minute bar beyond the band's far edge, margin 0) labels a hold that dips past line ± 12 for a full bar as broke (months 1–4: up to 6.6% of line-type holds). Test a cross margin for vwap, vwap_band, poc, lvn, em_level and va_edge of {4, 8} NQ pts (the band stays at its frozen width), judged on net R (the margin changes the structure label itself, so not on log loss), if the step-14 review cards or gate 16 show deep-dip holds being misjudged.

  - **Q-perfpage Performance page in the Control Panel (owner 2026-10-26). ✅ Decided and written into §9b (item 6, six pages) and §12 step 14 (a step-14 deliverable); docs only for now, no code and no `features_version` change.** Equity curves per account and for the model in R and $, one time axis, phases coloured with start markers (development, walk-forward 7–9, sealed, demo from step 24, each prop account from step 26); a stats panel (net R/$, trades, win rate, average and largest win/loss, profit factor, max drawdown close-to-close and intraday from stored paths, longest and average streaks); each prop account against its own drawdown limit with the closest approach; Monte Carlo of 1,000 day-block reshuffles (base, smallest and largest drawdown curves, a 5–95% band, the probability of reaching the drawdown limit, consistent with the §6.5b lives); live vs the backtest's band for the same trade count; an automated ledger guard (§7 rule 2b: months 7–9 only after the step-16 gate is recorded, sealed months only after the step-20 sealed score is recorded, else refused with the reason) with a unit test at step 14; data from the per-run trade ledgers, live logs and imported fills, no new pulls, CSV export. **Gate record (owner 2026-10-26, replacing the first proposal under `<reports_dir>`):** `config/gate_record.json` (committed in the repo, small, so it is not lost with the SSD; owner 2026-10-26): one entry per gate with the gate id, the date passed and the commit the gate was judged on (the parent of the commit that writes the entry, since a commit cannot hold its own hash); every gate approval appends its entry in the same commit as the CLAUDE.md Status "Last gate passed" line, so the two never disagree, and `tests/test_gate_record.py` checks the file parses and its latest gate matches that line; gate 6 (2026-10-05) back-filled (commit 07a312e, the approved feature spec; its Status line written by 5268b8a). The step-14 ledger guard reads it.
  - **Q-consistdip Consistency cap after a dip below the base (owner 2026-10-26). ✅ Decided and written into §6.1 (daily profit cap) and §6.7 (cap formula, `consistency_pct` comment, the Pro note, the simulator's pass test), `nqbot/risk.py` `consistency_cap_usd` with a test (`tests/test_risk.py`); account rules, not features: no `features_version` change.** Every `profit_cap_mode: consistency` stage: old cap = `consistency_pct` × `profit_target_usd`, fixed for the phase or payout cycle; new cap = `consistency_pct` × (`profit_target_usd` + max(0, base − lowest end-of-day balance so far)), base = the start balance in an evaluation, or the balance the current payout cycle is measured from in a funded stage. A dip below the base raises the cap; a loss that stays above the base does not (owner's rule). Examples: Rapid EOD eval never below $25,000 → $450; lowest EOD $24,500 → 30% × $2,000 = $600; Builder funded first cycle → $1,050; after a $300 dip below the base → 50% × $2,400 = $1,200; the Pro 50k evaluation uses the same formula (owner-confirmed 2026-10-26). The daily profit cap (§6.1), `stop_at_target` and the prop simulator's pass test all use it; nothing else in §6.7 changes. The owner's Q-vaedge answers of the same day: the three unstated rules (previous POC stays a chart level with the sweep on; `va_edge` flip margin in the chart-lines group; the RTH-anchored profile known from the 16:00 close, naked flag counted from then) are approved as set.
  - **Q-vaedge Previous-VA sweeps and the previous-profile anchor as step-13 tests (owner 2026-10-26). ✅ Decided and written into §5.3, §7.1 Tuning, §11, §12 step 13, feature_spec F3.2b / F3.9 / F12 / F16.4, features.yaml (`va_edge_sweep: false`, `prev_profile_anchor: globex_1800_1700`, `tune_grids_13.vaedge_variants`; `features_version` 20) and `features/sweep.py`, `features/prev_profile.py` with tests (`tests/test_vaedge_variants.py`).** Background: an outside backtest suggested both; its second year overlaps months 7–12, so nothing is adopted from it; the tests decide and the current rules stay unless clearly beaten. (1) `va_edge_sweep` {off (current), on}: on = `prev_vah` / `prev_val` sweep-eligible like highs/lows (§5.3: `sweep_max_depth`, `sweep_reclaim_bars`, sweep reclaim = style (c), the sweep extreme as a stop candidate, the swept features); scored like the other structure variants (events rebuilt on months 1–4, day-blocked cross-validated total net R per month with the bootstrap policy) on `va_edge` trades, trade counts reported (since 2026-11-04 decided on all trades of the run with this subset beside it, each fold's bar set on the shared events, Q-step12a (5)); off stays unless on is higher with the 95% day-block bootstrap interval of the difference excluding 0. (2) `prev_profile_anchor` {`globex_1800_1700` (current), `rth_0930_1600`}: which session the previous-session profile (POC, naked flag, VAH/VAL, the F3.12 value-context features) is built from; same scoring and keep-current rule on all events touching those levels, after (1) at its winner. Months 7–12 are never used. Notes from building it: with sweep on, `prev_poc` stays a chart level and the `va_edge` flip margin stays in the chart-lines group; with the RTH anchor the profile is known from that RTH close (an early close: `early_close_et`) and `prev_poc_naked` counts trades from then. (3) Question answered (no change): the prop-simulator rules of §6.7 (consistency, Builder's soft DLL, EOD trailing drawdown, minimum days) are specified in §6.7 / `accounts.yaml` fields but not built yet (the prop simulator is step 15); the owner re-checks the current MFFU terms.
  - **Q-areasp S&P entry features for area events (owner 2026-10-26). ✅ Decided and written into §5.4 (Area events, cross-market), §7 rule 5, §7.1 (Area-event inputs), feature_spec F2.19 / F5.8 / F5.16 / F16.8, features.yaml (`features_version` 17) and `features/area_cross.py` with tests (`tests/test_area_cross.py`); area events and their builder come at step 19, are not in the step-16 model, so nothing through the gate changes; they stay ablation group 5.** (1) Role: VAH-area event (short) = ceiling, VAL-area event (long) = floor. (2) Pair-scorer inputs only, at each pair's `decision_ts`, over [visit start, `decision_ts`]; the visit start = the moment NQ last entered the area on this visit; inside both the daily and the weekly area at the first firing: the earliest moment since which NQ has been continuously inside at least one of the areas containing it then; data ≤ `decision_ts`. (3) `es_at_level` (same column): at some τ in the window ES inside an ES level zone's band ± `es_zone_proximity` in role ρ, that level holding ρ at `decision_ts` (confirmed or provisional) and not crossed against ρ since its overlap (Q18); the same ES level set (dark pools out while `dp_in_model` is false). (4) Per S&P map: the same rule on relevant SPY/SPX nodes with the F2.19 rules (Q18, Q19, null in a dislocation block of that chain): `area_sp_aligned_spy` / `_spx`, the node's `area_sp_pct_of_king_<map>`, `area_sp_king_<map>` (confirmed King, Q28), `area_sp_gatekeeper_<map>` at its first overlap, and `area_sp_count` 0–2; `trinity_count` / `trinity_aligned` null for area events. (5) Which features area events get: §7.1 Area-event inputs (null, computed, and the unlisted ones placed by the same test). (6) Ablation: `es_at_level` for area events and every `area_sp_*` column in group 2 (Trinity / cross-market). (7) F10 / F3.6 front-contract wording (done 2026-10-26 with the zone-identity follow-up). Choices made while building it, **answered by the owner 2026-10-26 (`features_version` 18):** (i) with several aligned nodes on one map, the stats come from the node that overlapped first among those still counting at `decision_ts` (holding ρ, not crossed against ρ), a node that overlapped first but has since failed is skipped, a tie → the larger pct_of_king (test: A overlaps 10:01 and is crossed by 10:04, B overlaps 10:03 and holds, decision 10:05 → B); (ii) a dislocation block follows the level-event rule (F1.8, §13 Q-dislguard-2 (a)): `trinity_count` is listed among the null features, so `area_sp_count` is null while either S&P chain is blocked (the first build counted the blocked map as 0 and was changed to match), and the blocked map's columns are null; (iii) the overlap is checked at the visit start and at every ES trade, NQ trade and board apply in the window, as F2.19 does for level events (the first build's stretch-start shortcut replaced; tests: ES already inside at the visit start → counts; a node becoming relevant while ES is inside → counts from that board). **`es_at_level` during a block (owner 2026-10-26, `features_version` 19):** the narrower Q-dislguard-2 (a) rule, for level and area events alike (F1.8 fixed to match): true from an ES zone with no member from a blocked chain, else null if SPY or SPX was blocked in the window, else false (`features/es_level.py`, `tests/test_es_level.py`).
  - **Q-fibvariants Golden-zone orientation flip and 10:30 redraw as step-13 tests (owner 2026-10-26). ✅ Decided and written into §5.3, §5.4 (Containers), §5.8, §7.1 Tuning, §11, §12 step 13, feature_spec F3.6 / F10 / F12 / F16.4, features.yaml (`golden_zone_flip`, `golden_zone_redraw_1030`, both start true; `tune_grids_13.golden_zone_variants`; `features_version` 15) and `features/golden_zone.py` with tests (`tests/test_golden_zone.py`).** The flip and the redraw (Q-orfib) become pre-declared step-13 tests instead of fixed owner rules; `ib_fib_start_minute` (15), `ib_lock_minute` (30), the fib levels and the swing rule stay owner rules, never tuned. (1) Count report, months 1–4, counts and hold rates only: golden-zone structure-label hold rates and counts by `fib_flips` (0 vs ≥ 1) and by drawing (running before 10:00 / locked at 10:00 / redrawn at 10:30). (2) Variant test at step 13 on months 1–4, like the other structure parameters (§7.1): flip {on, off} × redraw {on, off}; each rebuilds the golden-zone events and is scored by day-blocked cross-validated total net R per month with the bootstrap policy on golden-zone trades only, trade counts reported (since 2026-11-04 decided on all trades of the run with this subset beside it, each fold's bar set on the shared events, Q-step12a (5)); the current design (on, on) stays unless a variant is higher with the 95% day-block bootstrap interval of the difference excluding 0; if several are, the largest lower bound wins; written with `frozen_from: months 1-4`; months 7–12 are never used. Flip off: the 0.79 invalidation ends the setup as now, the drawing never changes orientation, later returns re-arm on the original side (§5.4 Containers). Redraw off: the 10:00 drawing (and the IB high/low locked at 10:00) stays all day; the IB high/low follow the redraw switch (§5.3). Notes from building it (F10 Zone identity), answered by the owner 2026-10-26 (`features_version` 16): a new extreme before 10:00 keeps the `zone_id` only if the orientation stays the same; if the swing rule reverses it, it is a new golden zone, like a flip (the old zone's open event ends, pending entries removed with `exit`, touch count and freshness not carried over; tests: low 09:31 / high 09:40 / new low 09:52 → short, a new zone; a new high keeps the zone); a bar judges its open against the drawing at its open and its close against the drawing at its close (F0.5), so a zone made inside the bar can't be invalidated by it (approved as written). F10 and F3.6 now say the range is the max/min RTH trade on the session's front contract (F0.4; wording only).
- Step 13 (13a): confirm the NDXP ±20 band and freeze `em_level`'s half-width from the reaction-distance report (done 2026-11-10, Q-step13a-2: NDXP ±15, `em_level` 12); `dp_band` (0.5 QQQ $) is skipped while `dp_in_model` is false and set from the same report if dark-pool features ever enter the model (owner 2026-11-08, Q-step13plan (6)).
- By step 16 if it should gate step 16, otherwise any time: `gates.gate_pass_rate_min` (the evaluation pass rate the backtest must show; report-only until set).
- **Q-livedata How the live laptop reaches its data from step 21 (owner 2026-10-28; open, decided before step 21; owner's lean: B).** From step 11 the SSD is on the build PC (§11 Two machines). Live trading on the laptop needs `models/` (the production fit), `config/`, `paths.live_dir` (journal, `account_state.json`), the latest evening checkpoint `state_<date>.parquet` with its rolling statistics, the dark-pool print history (`dp_lookback_days`), today's gameplan, the calendar, and room for the live stream archives (`min_free_gb_live`); a missing data drive is an SSD failure for the live engine (§9). Retrains and top-ups need the full history on the PC. Options: **(A)** the SSD moves to the laptop at step 21: one copy, but the PC can't run retrains or top-ups unless it is carried back (weekends only); **(B)** a second SSD for the laptop (the live drive) with the models, live folder, checkpoints and recent raw data, the PC keeping the main SSD, and a nightly sync both ways (live archives → PC, new models → laptop) with a check that the copies agree: a drive to buy and a sync job to build; **(C)** the live folder on the laptop's internal drive (a few GB) with the same network sync: breaks "nothing on the internal drive" (needs an owner exception); **(D)** the PC shares the SSD over the LAN: live then depends on the network and the PC staying up (not recommended). With any option, the 08:30–16:15 job ban (§11 Overnight jobs) applies to the live machine only, and the two job-lock files must still stop two machines writing the same data.
- **Q-live-machine Whether the build PC may also be a live engine, as a backup or alternative to the laptop (owner 2026-11-09; open, decided before step 21).** The owner may want the PC as a backup or alternative live engine. Until this is decided the rule stands: the PC never trades, its `.env` holds no `TP_WEBHOOK_*` (`scripts/register_tasks_pc.ps1` refuses otherwise; §11 Two machines, CLAUDE.md rule 13). Before it is allowed, each of these is decided and, where it is code, built and tested: (1) **Security:** live TradersPost webhooks on the PC's Windows 10 Home 22H2, past end of support (no security updates since 2026-10-13; §11 Two machines): the options and their risk are written down for the owner (for example: keep the webhooks off the PC entirely; webhooks on the PC only while it is the live machine, put there from the password manager and removed after; a supported OS on the PC first), and the owner picks one; (2) **One engine live at a time:** a cross-machine lock so two engines (laptop and PC) can never send orders for the same account; the engine and guard lock files in `paths.live_dir` (§9) and the UI's same-webhook check (§10.1) are per machine and not enough; the lock, how a machine takes over after the other died, and its tests are specified with Q-livedata (where the live files live); (3) **No builds while it trades:** no feature build, retrain, top-up or other `nqbot run` job on the PC during market hours while it is the live machine (the 08:30–16:15 ET ban of §11 Overnight jobs applied to it, enforced as on the laptop); (4) **Checks repeated on the PC:** the step-23 replay and latency checks, the guard process and the Discord remote are run again and verified on the PC before it may trade; (5) **Control Panel:** the Control Panel's automatic Calm effects apply on whichever machine is live ("Calm" is the owner's term; it isn't defined in this SPEC yet and is specified with the step-14 Control Panel, §9b).
- **Q-frontroll Which contract is the front around a roll (owner 2026-10-30). ✅ Decided (owner 2026-10-30) and written into §3.2 Front month, §5.1 Same contract, §12 step 8, feature_spec F0.4 and `features_version` 26 (nothing built under 25 reads it; the step-8 bars and roll spreads are rebuilt).** Before: the front was Databento's `NQ.v.0` / `ES.v.0` mapping for the CME day. Measured from stored data (volumes only, no prices; every roll of the window, a data-coverage measure under §7 rule 2b): v.0 switches on the Wednesday of expiry week (NQ 2025-09-18, 2025-12-17, 2026-03-18, 2026-06-17, 2026-09-16; ES the same except 2025-09-17), 4 sessions after the CME roll date (the Thursday 8 days before expiry). On the last session before each switch the old contract held 13–20% of the two contracts' CME-day volume (e.g. 2025-12-16 NQZ5 115,614 vs NQH6 545,470; 2026-03-17 NQH6 77,381 vs NQM6 354,897), and on the Monday before it the old contract's volume was already a third to a half of its normal level, so the bot read the thin, expiring contract for about 2–3 sessions per roll (data, features, levels, R and the live tickers). **Decision (owner 2026-10-30):** (1) the previous-session volume rule: `front_by_volume()` decides the front for CME day T from each contract's volume on the previous full session (18:00–17:00); a holiday Globex day never triggers a switch and keeps the previous session's contract; the candidate is always the next quarterly, never switched back; one function for backtest and live, live in an evening step after 17:00 ET before T starts at 18:00; roll days, roll spreads (new − old at 17:00 of the new T−1) and the §3.5/§10.3 roll-day ticker handling all derive from it; NQ and ES. (2) The `ohlcv-1h` pull of the candidate contracts ($0.00 by `get_cost`) approved; the switch-day table is shown to the owner before anything else is pulled. (3) Pre-approved up to $3.00 in total, re-priced right before: the new-contract files of the moved days only (NQ and ES `trades` and `ohlcv-1s`, MNQ `trades`, NQ `mbp-1` 09:25–16:05), the Sep 2025 warm-up roll included. (4) Then rebuilt: the 1- and 5-minute bars of the moved days, all 10 roll spreads, `databento condition` on the new files, the dislocation count for the moved December and March days and the gate-8b job for 2026-03-17 on M6; a frozen gate-8b value (stop floors, Atlas tolerances) that moves is shown to the owner, never changed. (5) Tests: switch on the first day the new contract's previous-session volume is larger; no switch back if the volumes cross again; a holiday Globex day doesn't trigger a switch; day T's volume never decides day T (`tests/test_databento_contracts.py`). Code: `front_by_volume`, `clients/databento_volume.py`, `nqbot databento volumes`; a re-plan that puts a day on another contract moves that day's bars and the moved rolls' spreads to `processed/replaced/front_rule/` so the job rebuilds them. The rule needs the next quarterly's volume on every session, so the hourly bars cover the three nearest unexpired quarterlies each session (between a switch and the old contract's expiry the new front's own next quarterly is the third). Open for step 22: where the live evening step reads both candidates' T−1 volume (Databento's historical `ohlcv-1h` after 17:00, as the backtest, or the live feed subscribed to both in roll weeks), and what it does if they can't be read before 18:00. Follow-ups (owner 2026-10-30): `step8b recorded-day` decides its days' contracts by the same `front_by_volume` (its chain starts after the last quarterly expiry, hourly bars pulled at $0 or refused); the six October recorded days (10-07, 10-09, 10-12, 10-15, 10-16, 10-19) stay on NQZ6/ESZ6, as under v.0. Step 12's roll-week day is 2026-03-17 in place of 03-18. **Done 2026-10-30:** the switch-day table (owner-approved) is the one `nqbot databento volumes` prints: every roll one session before v.0 and 3 sessions after the CME roll date (NQ U5→Z5: 4); the deciding volumes in the plan output. Hourly bars $0 (16 contracts, plus 10-01…10-19 for the recorded days). Re-plan with `--approve-usd 0.80`: 28 requests, pay-as-you-go NQZ5 trades 2025-09-17 $0.458 and ESZ5 trades 2025-09-16 $0.338 by `get_cost` ($0.796), the rest $0; 30 derived files moved aside (the moved days' 1- and 5-minute bars, the 10 old spreads). Roll spreads (new − old at 17:00 ET of the new T−1), old in brackets: NQ 2025-09-17 237.75 (242.25), 12-16 247.25 (236.75), 2026-03-17 217.50 (219.75), 06-16 302.00 (314.00), 09-15 296.00 (290.00); ES 2025-09-16 57.50 (56.75), 12-16 57.25 (55.00), 2026-03-17 50.50 (51.25), 06-16 65.25 (67.75), 09-15 67.75 (67.00). `databento condition`: the same 10 degraded dates, the new files' completeness normal. Dislocation days 2025-12-16 and 2026-03-17 re-run on the new contracts: per-board events at 0.05% SPY 7 → 4 (12-16), QQQ 3 → 1 and SPX 6 → 5 (03-17; SPX in force too), none at 0.1%/0.2% changed, the guard summary unchanged (QQQ 5 blocks, 2 false fires, 0.083 a week; SPY 1; SPX 2). Gate 8b with 03-17 on NQM6/ESM6: every condition passes (QQQ p95 4.041 vs best 4.044 NQ, SPY 0.795 vs 0.798, SPX 0.784 vs 0.783 ES; bias QQQ 0.022 [−0.015, 0.060]), no frozen value moves (QQQ floor 4.25, Atlas tolerances 4.05 NQ / 0.80 ES, NDXP 6.50 from the unchanged October recordings).
